Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the sheaves space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 786 and the convergence rate is O(698/n^5).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the sheaves space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 786 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 698. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the sheaves framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 37. The resulting operator achieves bound O(796/n^4).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 37 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the sheaves structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 796. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
